{"forms":[{"form":"histories","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"Hx","tags":["alternative"]},{"form":"hx","tags":["alternative"]},{"form":"historie","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]},{"form":"hystorie","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]},{"form":"hystory","tags":["alternative","nonstandard"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The history of mammals stretches back many millions of years.","type":"example"},{"text":"History repeats itself if we don’t learn from its mistakes.","type":"example"},{"text":"He dreams of an invention that will make history.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The aggregate of past events, both unrecorded and recorded."],"synonyms":[{"word":"past"},{"word":"ages"},{"word":"background"}],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He teaches Latin American history at the university and publishes books about the Cold War.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The empirical study of past events, as distinct from literature, myth, or scripture; the assessment of notable events."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"in all of human history and prehistory","type":"example"},{"text":"since the dawn of history","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The portion of the past that is known and recorded by this field of study, as opposed to all earlier and unknown times that preceded it."],"synonyms":[{"word":"ages"}],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"a long and sordid history","type":"example"},{"text":"What is your medical history?","type":"example"},{"text":"The family's history includes events best forgotten.","type":"example"},{"text":"This car has something of a history attached to it.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A set of events involving an entity."],"tags":["countable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I really enjoyed Shakespeare's tragedies more than his histories.","type":"example"},{"text":"a short history of post-Columbian colonization","type":"example"},{"text":"Thucydides wrote a history of the Peleponnesian War that uses different sources than Herodotus' work.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A record or narrative description of past events."],"synonyms":[{"word":"account"},{"word":"chronicle"},{"word":"story"},{"word":"tale"}],"tags":["countable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"A personal medical history is required for the insurance policy.","type":"example"},{"text":"He has a history of cancer in his family.","type":"example"},{"text":"This diagnosis is usually based solely on the history and physical examination, although laboratory tests are occasionally also obtained.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A list of past and continuing medical conditions of an individual or family."],"synonyms":[{"word":"medical history"}],"tags":["countable"],"topics":["medicine","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I visited a great site yesterday but forgot the URL. Luckily, I didn't clear my history.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A record of previous user events, especially of visited web pages in a browser."],"synonyms":[{"word":"log"}],"tags":["countable"],"topics":["computing","engineering","mathematics","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I told him that if he doesn't get his act together, he's history.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Something that no longer exists or is no longer relevant."],"tags":["countable","informal","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"There is too much history between them for them to split up now.","type":"example"},{"text":"He has had a lot of history with the police.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Shared experience or interaction."],"tags":["uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"You are witnessing history!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A historically significant event."],"tags":["uncountable"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"hĭsʹt(ə)rē"},{"ipa":"/ˈhɪs.tə.ɹi/","tags":["Australia","Canada","General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈhɪs.tɹi/","tags":["Australia","Canada","General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈhɪst͡ʃ(ə)ɹɪj/","tags":["British","Southern","Standard"]},{"ipa":"/ˈhɪst(ə)ɹɪj/","tags":["British","Southern","Standard"]},{"ipa":"/ˈhəs.tə.ɹi/","tags":["New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"/ˈhəs.tɹi/","tags":["New-Zealand"]}],"upstream_line":158661,"upstream_offset":734404809,"upstream_record_sha256":"b92c689c8385b3ca409b894401b6e70b93afa07b06be38c04210bb108c9ec7ab","word":"history"}
